USA_recovery_decisions = { #自废墟中崛起
	#内战后重建地区总开关
	USA_rebuild_alaska_2 = { #重建阿拉斯加
		name = USA_rebuild_alaska
		icon = generic_political_discourse
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				is_acw_tag = yes
				is_acw_minor = yes
			}
		}
		available = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = USA_currently_rebuilding }
			463 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		visible = {
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = USA_open_csa_decision	
					has_country_flag = USA_open_cor_decision	
					has_country_flag = USA_open_nee_decision	
					has_country_flag = USA_open_tex_decision	
					has_country_flag = USA_open_wcc_decision	
					has_country_flag = USA_open_cal_decision	
				}	
			}
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					463 = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					1273 = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					1348 = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					1349 = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					1350 = { is_core_of = ROOT }
				}
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 10
		days_remove = 30
		ai_will_do = { factor = 100 }
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove USA_rebuild_alaska_2"
			clr_country_flag = USA_currently_rebuilding
			every_owned_state = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { is_core_of = ROOT }
					OR = {
						state = 463
						state = 1273
						state = 1348
						state = 1349
						state = 1350
					}
				}
				remove_ACW_cores = yes
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision USA_rebuild_alaska_2"
			set_country_flag = USA_currently_rebuilding
		}
	}

		}
		fire_only_once = yes

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove USA_rebuild_hawaii_2"
			clr_country_flag = USA_currently_rebuilding
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state = 629
						state = 632
						state = 631
						state = 642
						state = 630
						state = 726
						state = 727
					}
				}
				remove_ACW_cores = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = HAW
						NOT = { country_exists = HAW }
					}
					remove_core_of = HAW
				}
				add_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision USA_rebuild_hawaii_2"
			set_country_flag = USA_currently_rebuilding
		}

	USA_reclaim_new_england_decision_2 = { #收复新英格兰
		name = USA_reclaim_new_england_decision
		icon = generic_political_discourse
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				is_acw_tag = yes
				is_acw_minor = yes
			}
		}
		available = {
			is_subject = no
			controls_all_owned_states = yes
			if = {
				limit = { country_exists = NEE }
				NEE = {
					controls_all_owned_states = yes
					is_neighbor_of = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					CAN = {
						OR = {
							owns_state = 357 #Maine
							owns_state = 934 #New Hampshire
							owns_state = 933 #Vermont
							owns_state = 932 #Massachusetts
							owns_state = 935 #Rhode Island
							owns_state = 931 #Connecticut
							owns_state = 796 #Upstate New York
						}
					}
				}
				CAN = {
					controls_all_owned_states = yes
					is_neighbor_of = ROOT
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			932 = {
				is_claim_or_core_of_ROOT = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = CAN
				has_war_with = NEE
				count_triggers = {
					amount = 2
					country_exists = USA
					country_exists = CAL
					country_exists = TEX
					country_exists = CSA
					country_exists = COR
					country_exists = WCC
					AND = {
						country_exists = NEE
						NEE = { 
							has_country_flag = NEE_claimed_america
							is_ally_of_ROOT = no
						}
					}
					AND = {
						country_exists = TXS
						TXS = { 
							has_country_flag = TXS_claimed_america
							is_ally_of_ROOT = no
						}
					}
					AND = {
						country_exists = NYC
						NYC = {
							has_country_flag = NYC_can_invade_america
							is_ally_of_ROOT = no
						}
					}
					AND = {
						country_exists = BBR
						BBR = {	
							has_country_flag = BBR_claimed_america
							is_ally_of_ROOT = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			OR = {
				NEE = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject_of = CAN
				}
				CAN = {
					OR = {
						owns_state = 357 #Maine
						owns_state = 934 #New Hampshire
						owns_state = 933 #Vermont
						owns_state = 932 #Massachusetts
						owns_state = 935 #Rhode Island
						owns_state = 931 #Connecticut
						owns_state = 796 #Upstate New York
					}
				}
			}
			has_global_flag = USA_civil_war_over_flag
			OR = {
				is_acw_minor = no
				acw_minor_claimed_america = yes
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 80

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				can_declare_war_on = CAN
				ROOT_is_threatened = yes
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				can_declare_war_on = CAN
				CAN = { default_attack_AI_trigger_FROM = yes }
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision USA_reclaim_new_england_decision_2"
			CAN = {
				country_event = USA.1505
			}
		}
	}

					NOT = { has_completed_focus = USA_hawking_plan }
				}

				custom_effect_tooltip = USA_ODP_gains_strength2
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { var = SPA_strength value = 3 }
					add_to_variable = { var = AFP_strength value = 3 }
					add_to_variable = { var = ODP_strength value = 3 }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = USA_new_industrial_act }

				custom_effect_tooltip = USA_AFP_gains_strength
				custom_effect_tooltip = USA_SPA_gains_strength
				custom_effect_tooltip = USA_ODP_gains_strength

					add_to_variable = { var = AFP_strength value = 2 }
					add_to_variable = { var = ODP_strength value = 2 }
				}
			}

USA_MacArthur_policies = { #麦克阿瑟政策
	USA_mac_retreats = { #撤退到西部军指挥中心
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_independence
		allowed = { original_tag = USA }
		available = { has_government = paternal_autocrat 
		surrender_progress > 0.90
		NOT = {
		WCC = {
		has_war_with = USA
				}
			}
		USA = { #美利坚合众国国策树
		has_War = yes
			}
		}
		visible = {
		WCC = {
		exists = yes
			} 
		}
		cost = 0
		fire_only_once = yes

		ai_will_do = { factor = 40 }

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision USA_mac_retreats"
			WCC = {
				add_manpower = 20000
				country_event = {
					id = wcc.11
					days = 7
				}
				country_event = {
					id = wcc.1101
					days = 10
				}
			}
			USA = { #美利坚合众国国策树
				every_unit_leader = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_id = 1408
							has_id = 2202
						}
					}
					set_nationality = WCC
				}
				every_navy_leader = {
					set_nationality = WCC
				}
				if = {
					else = {
						annex_country = {
							target = USA
							transfer_troops = no
						}
					}
				}
			}
			
		}
	}
}

			NOT = { is_in_tech_sharing_group = us_research }
			FROM = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 0

		target_root_trigger = { is_in_faction = yes }
		targets = { USA CAL TEX WCC NEE BBR TXS ALA NYC HAW PRI ARZ NVN LIB }
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_faction_leader = yes
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
			}
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision USA_join_iadc target: [From.GetName]"
			add_to_tech_sharing_group = us_research
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						TEX = { is_faction_leader = yes }
						NOT = { has_idea = TEX_research_deals }
					}
					add_ideas = TEX_research_deals
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						TEX = { 
							is_faction_leader = yes
							has_completed_focus = TEX_mutual_trade
						}
						NOT = { has_idea = TEX_trade_deals }
					}
					add_ideas = TEX_trade_deals
				}
			}
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
		}
	}
}
